Here, regarding this question, people also blame the **Bohemians** and say: one must surely see that the **Bohemians** have committed a **crime of high treason** <note>original: *crimen læsæ Majestatis*; the crime of violating the majesty of the sovereign.</note>, for to carry out their expulsion, they raised a considerable army against the **Emperor**.

Now, we wish to decide this question with a righteous judgment, known to all the world, and through God's grace. First, when the **Bohemians** threw their opponents out of the window <note>A reference to the Defenestration of Prague on May 23, 1618, where Protestant nobles threw two Imperial governors out of a window of Prague Castle, signaling the start of the Thirty Years' War.</note>, a riot arose among the common people, who briefly wanted to kill all the **Papists** <note>A contemporary term for Roman Catholics.</note>. But the noble **Count of Thurn** <note>Heinrich Matthias von Thurn (1567–1640), one of the primary leaders of the Bohemian Revolt.</note> exerted himself to the utmost upon his horse, vehemently begging and pleading with the mob that they should not harm even the lowliest of the **Catholics**, but instead remain peaceful until matters were settled. Oh, how it would have gone for those **Papists** then, if the aforementioned noble Count had not been their angel, second only to **God**! Truly, the sensible and honorable **Catholics** still publicly praise him for this.

Furthermore, when they took up arms for their **defense** <note>original: *Defension*; the legal and military act of protecting their chartered rights.</note>, no one can say with a good conscience that they attempted any aggression—much less against the **Emperor**. For if one never offended the **Catholics**, how can one claim an offense against the **Emperor**? Let a sensible person consider how shamefully we **Bohemians** are being lied about! However, what happened against the **Jesuits** <note>original: *Jesuwüten*; a polemical German pun combining "Jesuit" with "Wüten" (raging/fury).</note>—that, they truly deserved. If the **Bohemians** had wanted to undertake something violent against the **Emperor**, oh, how easily they could have done so with such convenient opportunities! But no; they showed restraint until the very end. Indeed, a report truly went out from the **Emperor's** own officers, saying that if they had held such good opportunities in their hands as the **Bohemians** did, they would have long since overcome them all.

Yes, they were so careful to avoid shedding human blood and taking action against the **Emperor** that the entire kingdom became dissatisfied and everyone cried out: "**By delaying too much, victory is lost**" <note>original: *Nimium cunctãdo victoria amittitur*</note>. The **Bohemian** lords delay so long that they will finally be completely overcome—which then actually happened. But it was thus God's providence and will. Nevertheless, in this matter, the **Bohemians** fairly respected the **Emperor** and always proceeded gently, until finally the other side also applied themselves with vehemence.

But if the **Bohemians** had been as greedy as the **Papacy**, oh, what blows they would have dealt out! But no, the general...

<unclear>Con</unclear>stant and documented **Truth** testifies much to the contrary; therefore the Bohemians did not take up arms <note>original: *ad arma*; to arms</note> to force the Emperor, but rather established a Defense <note>original: *Defension*; a legal and military standing to protect constitutional rights.</note> against their enemies and those of the entire Fatherland.

How bloodthirsty the Papists <note>A contemporary, often derogatory term for Roman Catholics.</note> are on the other hand is testified by the persecutions against the Evangelicals <note>Protestants, specifically those following the Augsburg Confession.</note>, both old and new, where even the dead were not spared. I cannot wonder enough how those who only *claim* to be Evangelicals think they should still help such inhuman tyrants.

Whether the Bohemians have now also taken up a proper Defense with a good conscience is shown by their privileges of governance; it is shown by the worldly and natural rule that everyone has the power to defend themselves against their enemy, especially against one who is of a different faith and religion. But our enemies blaspheme and say: "Oh, what a fine conscience the Bohemians have! The outcome has testified what kind of luck and blessing they had—namely, that they have become the shame and mockery of the whole world." Yes, it is true that the Bohemians are overcome, but for their best fortune and their enemies' greatest misfortune; and because the game is far from over, one will shortly experience something else that one never would have expected. The sensible man knows that the righteous must suffer much, but no longer than God wills; for He knows well how to protect and rescue His own at the right time, but keeps the unrighteous for punishment until the day of judgment. 2 Peter 2:9. In the meantime, you slanderers <note>original: *Calumnianten*; those who spread false and malicious statements.</note> might mock bravely; it will surely come to pass when it strikes twenty-three by the Bohemian clock <note>The "Bohemian clock" or "Italian hours" counted 24 hours from sunset; "twenty-three" signifies the final hour before the end of the day or a coming darkness.</note>; then a crowd and night will come upon our enemies, so that they too will have to go to sleep. If it then does not happen, then a lying spirit will have spoken out of me.

But the laudable Estates <note>original: *Stände*; the representative political classes of the kingdom.</note> had fair cause and right to set up a Defense, especially because the Jesuitical swine wallowed into the government and rooted around in it with their snouts. But they, those asses, have reached for the Emperor’s crown and scepter—not we Bohemians. From this, the Archbishop had to command over the Evangelicals, which did not suit him at all; and one knows well how the Jesuits deal with the potentates, and how they [lead] them to Un[just...]

### V.

## Whether the Bohemians, with a good conscience and for political reasons, rejected **FERDINAND** and elected **FREDERICK** the Elector as King?

This is now once again a clamorous shouting and slandering; for the simple-minded say that one can see and grasp what the "honorable Bohemians" have done: first, they set their crowned King **FERDINAND** upon the throne, and then they thrust him down again. Whether this was right for the Bohemians is testified not only by those who are wise but also by the outcome. Now, you dear slanderers, let me also speak of this.

Since the Bohemians have rejected **FERDINAND**, there must be a reason why they did so. This, however, is the cause: namely, during the election of **FERDINAND**, the principal grievance of the Evangelicals <note>Protestants, specifically those adhering to the Augsburg Confession or the Reformed faith.</note> was the concern they felt regarding this election, especially because **FERDINAND** had practiced a cruel tyranny in his own lands and, from his youth, preferred to keep Jesuits near him who incited him toward bloodbaths. Even though the opposing party promised much, the Jesuit rule remained in their hearts, which states: *Faith need not be kept with heretics* <note>original: *Hæretica non est servanda fides*. This was a common Protestant polemic accusing Catholics of believing that legal promises made to Protestants were non-binding.</note>.

If we speak further of the election, we must also consider whether it is to be recognized as a legitimate election, and whether it occurred with the consent of the superiors and all others. But no, there one finds a very different and widely known method of choosing <note>original: *eligere*; to elect or choose.</note>. For as soon as the proposition was delivered by Emperor **MATTHIAS**, they immediately moved into that "Babylonian Council"—regardless of whether it was occupied by high or low-ranking men—and the Evangelical parties were almost [forced] into giving their consent...

they wanted to proceed with the pretext that "the majority concludes" <note>original Latin: *maxima pars concludit*</note>, as there were many Papists present. When people excused themselves against this—namely, explaining how in this business one must take into account not only the customs of the land but also the incorporated lands, Silesia and Moravia, as they are the foremost members of the Kingdom—it was ignored. Also, several deputies of the Third Estate from the cities excused themselves, claiming they must first seek counsel and consent from their home councils, for they had no mandate to consent to the election; they knew nothing about it beforehand, and nothing had been announced regarding a new election.

Do you mockers think the Bohemians notice and understand nothing at all, as if one had to ask you first? Who wouldn't grasp that the Papists always bypass everyone with their schemes—indeed, those devilish scorpion tails and abyssal locusts, the Jesuits! They were the ones who wanted to push the highly praised Emperor and King **RUDOLF** (of gracious memory) from the royal throne with a violent hand, using their [Archduke] Leopold and the Passauers.

### V.

## Whether the Bohemians, with a good conscience and for political reasons, rejected FERDINAND and elected FREDERICK the Elector as King?

Now, once again, there is a loud outcry and slandering, for the ignorant say that one need only look at what the honorable Bohemians have done: they first placed their crowned King **FERDINAND** upon the throne and then pushed him back down again. Whether this was right for the Bohemians is testified not only by any sensible person but also by the final outcome. Now, you dear slanderers, let me also speak of this.

Since the Bohemians have now rejected **FERDINAND**, there must be a reason why they did so. This is the reason: at the election of **FERDINAND**, the primary grievance of the Protestants <note>The author uses the term *Evangelischen* to refer to the Lutheran and Reformed parties in Bohemia.</note> concerned the nature of the election itself, especially because **FERDINAND** practiced a cruel tyranny in his own lands and, from his youth, surrounded himself with Jesuits who incited him toward a bloodbath. Although the opposing side promised much, the Jesuit rule nonetheless remains in their hearts, which states: <term>No faith should be kept with heretics</term> <note>original Latin: *Haeretico non est servanda fides*; this was a frequent Protestant polemic, accusing Catholics of believing that treaties with non-Catholics were not binding.</note>. One is supposedly not required to keep one's word to a heretic.

But no, we find something far different and a more notorious <note>original German: *Landtkündiger*, implying a scandal widely known throughout the territory.</note> method of choosing. For as soon as the proposition was delivered by Emperor **MATTHIAS**, they immediately followed the "Babylonian counsel" — whether it was attended by many or few — and forced the Protestant parties, as it were, into a consent —

It happened just as it did with Rehoboam <note>A biblical reference to King Rehoboam (1 Kings 12), whose harshness and rejection of wise counsel led to the rebellion of the ten tribes of Israel.</note>. For the scorpion tails of the "Jesuwits" <note>original: *Jesuwüten*. From this, you sensible people, render a righteous judgment, so that you do not perish with the mockers. One would have to have great trust in that "blasphemous horn" <note>likely a reference to the "little horn" of Daniel 7, representing a prideful power that speaks against the Most High, here applied to the Catholic faction</note>; it has surely kept its faith well in the past! 

Pray, tell me someone, where is the Imperial Capitulation? Furthermore, it is well known what a fine business has always been made of "elections" <note>original: *electionen*</note>. Tell me, someone, what did the Passau Invasion of **1611** <note>A military intervention by Archduke Leopold of Austria with mercenaries from Passau, intended to seize power from his brother, Emperor Rudolf II.</note> mean? Do you mockers think the Bohemians can notice and understand nothing at all, and that one must ask you first?

GOD hears; He is unchanging <note>original: *unverreut*; likely a variant of *unbereut*, meaning God does not regret His promises or change His mind</note>. ## VII.

### Whether the Union is from GOD, where its foundation lies in Scripture, and what is to be held of it in all respects:

An honor-forgetting, self-proclaimed "Evangelical" slanderer <term>Calumniant</term> <note>a person who engages in calumny or malicious misrepresentation</note> made himself heard at the New Year of this current year, with a speech of praise, greeting, and well-wishing for the Noble, Magnanimous Eagle <note>The Imperial Eagle, symbol of the Holy Roman Emperor Ferdinand II</note>, etc. This fellow exceedingly paints the Eagle and the little wreath of cabbage <note>original: *Krauten Kränzlein*; likely a mocking reference to a heraldic device or a minor decoration</note>, while conversely mocking the Union <note>The Protestant Union, a coalition of Protestant German states formed in 1608</note> and our King wackerly, with such foolish fantasy that one can clearly see how the ass has been shot with hare-shot and scorched with great fox-tails <note>original: *Fuchsschwänzen*; in the 17th century, "to stroke with a fox-tail" was a metaphor for flattery, hypocrisy, or deceptive speech</note> that hang thick upon him. Thus, it would be no wonder if wings grew out of them, like those of the Eagle, carrying this bald-pate <note>original: *Kahlaten*; a derogatory term likely referring to a tonsured Catholic priest or a specific court official</note> alive into the Hell in Heaven on account of his beautiful speech of praise.

But how this donkey-like fox and thief of honor—who hides his name like a mouse in a hole and only peeps out—intends to act against and harass the Union, I will mention here. When the hideous slanderer has driven his donkey-like fantasy through two pages concerning the Eagle and its opponent, he also comes upon the Union. He calls it a "patched-together beggar’s coat" <note>original: *zusammengeslickten Bettelmansrock*; suggesting the Protestant Union is a fragile, mismatched, and impoverished alliance</note> which one must and should wonder at, and he uses the reversal of the word **Union** and says: *no i nu* <note>a mocking wordplay or phonetic reversal intended to suggest the Union is fleeting or nonsensical</note>. "What will become of this?" he asks, "that they set up such a strange alliance without GOD’S command? Therefore, with this union, it may easily happen according to the word of the name, and in a *no i nu* [in a moment] it can be torn apart." And so it is just as the Psalm says: *The kings of the earth set themselves up, and the rulers take counsel together against the LORD and against His Anointed* <note>Psalm 2:2; the author ironically flips the critic's biblical citation back against the Imperial side</note>, etc. He further calls this alliance a fox-like, straw-like, and "hare’s alliance" <note>implying the members are cowardly or the bond is weak as straw</note> that will hold together and stand just as a hare stands by its young, etc.

Now then, Mr. Bleline <note>A satirical or anagrammatic nickname for the author's opponent, likely Matthias Hoë von Hoënegg</note>, now we shall speak gallantly with one another and catch a remarkably strange fox, who shall look just like a great, coarse donkey.

First, I would like to know whether this same fox has not read my **Prophetic Decision** <note>original: *Prophetische Decisionem*; likely a reference to a previous pamphlet where the author interpreted current events through prophecy</note>, in which I showed and testified to the foundation of the Union quite differently from the **Revelation** <note>original: *ex Apocalypsi*</note>. And because he might not have grasped it yet, I will paint it once more upon his donkey-nose and stuff his donkey-pranks down his throat until he chokes on them.

Concerning now the praiseworthy **Union** <term>Protestant Union</term> <note>A coalition of Protestant German states formed in 1608 for self-defense</note> of Princes, it was in no way directed against the **Holy Roman Empire**, but rather against the **adulterous Whore of Rome** <note>A common Reformation-era epithet for the Papacy, referencing the Whore of Babylon in Revelation</note> and against those who commit whoredom with her. This is how the Union is constituted: The Papists have established a **Holy League** <note>original: *Sanctam ligam*; refers to the Catholic League formed in 1609 to counter the Protestant Union</note> against the Evangelicals, and thus they themselves have made and started a division in the Roman Empire based on the **Jesuitical Rule**. In response, the Evangelicals have also established a covenant and Union against the Pope, and one is worth the other. If the Papists have made no division in the Empire with their **Shameful League** <note>original: *Schanta liga*; a mocking pun on *Santa Liga* (Holy League) using the German word *Schande* for shame</note>, then the Union has done so even less; one is like the other, and the members of the Union are even less guilty than the Papists. 

But it wonders me that this chosen, self-proclaimed "Evangelical" slanderer <term>Calumniant</term> dares to act against the members of the Union, when even a fool should have read how the Pope must finally be rooted out along with all his adulterers. This slanderer's Lord, however, does not hold with the Union, neither **openly** <note>original: *ex professo*</note> nor secretly; now, he should also not hold with the **Shameful League**, but he is **in the middle** <note>original: *in medio*</note>, believing himself to be **most safe** <note>original: *tutissimus*</note>. But he does not know what Christ says of him: how he is **lukewarm** <note>original: *Law*; Early Modern German for lukewarm</note> and neither cold nor warm, **Revelation 3:16**. Nevertheless, he stands by the Pope **openly** <note>original: *ex professo*</note> and believes such a thing to be right **by law and in fact** <note>original: *de jure unnd de facto*</note>, helping to briskly execute the Saints of the Most High. 

The slanderer need not worry himself at all about the Union, for we do not ask what kind of alliance the **House of Austria** <note>The Habsburg Dynasty</note> made with **Electoral Saxony** in the year 1618 against the LORD and his anointed—which has now come quite clearly to light. The slanderer should concern himself much more with that.

For thus it is written in **Revelation 17:16-17**: "And the ten horns which you saw upon the beast..."

Is the highly praiseworthy **Union** <note>The Protestant Union, a coalition of German Protestant states formed in 1608 for self-defense</note> still a **beggar’s cloak** <note>A derogatory term Hoë used to imply the Union was a flimsy, worthless protection</note> to you? O, you desperate, wicked boy, why do you call so many noble Princes "hares," "foxes," or "**Calvinist straw-incendiaries**," <note>Hoë was a "Gnesio-Lutheran" who despised Calvinists, often using animal metaphors to mock their perceived cowardice or treachery</note> and the like? Do you think you have handled yourself well in this? 

Well, take heed: as one shouts into the woods, so it echoes back. If you fine "nobles" had not blasphemed, the opposing side would not have to do so either. But I shame no one except through the **Scriptures**, to which I testify by God's grace—and for which I am sent—so that I must bear witness to the testimony of **JESUS CHRIST**. Nothing can prevent this; whether it vexes the Devil or his mother, the Beast and the Dragon, truth must remain truth, even if there were seven **Doctor Hoës** against it. For if you honor-forgetting, frivolous fellows had not challenged me, I would not have to defend myself. If it applies to me, it applies to you; if it applies to you, it applies to me.

But since the **Union** does not please Doctor Hoë either—in that he says potentates should rightly hesitate to enter into it—I will herewith rebuke him and show what an unskilled and unlearned **oaf** he is. He lets his scribblings concerning the **Apocalypse** <note>The Book of Revelation</note> go abroad, yet he has not seen or read enough therein to know how, rightfully, in a joined **Union**, both the **Pope**, the **Jezebel**, and all her **adulterous kings** on earth shall be executed. 

That is what it means to be a "Doctor of Theology": when one understands the Scriptures so "beautifully" that he does not yet know how the **Emperor** is the adulterer of the **Whore of Babylon** <note>Protestant reformers commonly identified the Roman Catholic Church with the "Whore of Babylon" and the Pope with the Antichrist</note> in Rome, and shall be burned together with her. That is some "studying"—when one gives counsel to his Prince to stand by the Emperor, even though it is described in **Revelation 13 and Daniel 7** how he blasphemes God in Heaven and destroys His saints. He has not yet read that all those who take the **Mark of the Beast** shall also drink from the same cup as the Beast. 

You **lazy ass**, read now the 13th and 17th chapters of **Revelation**; do me the favor **for the sake of our shared studies**, <note>original: "propter communia studia"</note> as I might say; I will also read the text better for your sake. Oh, how you sell the noble blood of Princes! How terribly you will stand before the grim judgment seat of **JESUS CHRIST**. I have [called] you out of God's bles-

If you wish to act for others, then do so from the ground of Scripture / and not from your own whim / like the fools and hares / otherwise the fox's tail will be mowed off / so that one may see he is a hare / and a long-eared donkey. Finally, I beg you, you fire-foxes and hares / that you will henceforth be at peace with the **Union** / and never mock it / For / though one may think / it shall be torn apart again / yet it is nothing / but it must yet again be truly confirmed and strengthened anew. Let nothing lead you astray / everything that resists God must yet go to the gallows.

<meta>This page continues the author’s polemical attack on Doctor Matthias Hoë von Hoënegg. VIII.

# Whether one should, according to a political, external conscience, respect the authorities and, against one's inner conscience, help to persecute fellow believers.

This is truly not the least of questions among others; for the **Calvinists** <note>Followers of John Calvin. The author is likely a "Reformed" or Calvinist Protestant arguing against the "High Lutheran" position of the Dresden court</note>, namely the so-called **Evangelicals** <note>In this period, "Evangelical" was the general term for Protestants, though here used specifically for those the author defends</note>, cry out tremendously that one must serve and help the **Emperor** for conscience's sake—as if no one knew or had a conscience except for these poor mockers. But you fellows should know that another Prince has just as much of a conscience as you do; you haven't swallowed it all yourselves <note>original: *ihr habts nicht allein gefressen*; an idiom meaning "you don't have a monopoly on it"</note>. And I will, through God's grace, tell you much better what a conscience is than that poor bungler **Doctor Matz** <note>A mocking nickname for Matthias Hoë von Hoënegg (1580–1645), the influential and controversial Chief Court Preacher in Dresden who advocated for an alliance with the Catholic Emperor against other Protestants</note> in **Dresden**.

First, we want to look at this matter of conscience from the **Scriptures**, and not according to the brains of **fire-foxes** or **hares** <note>Continuing the animal insults from the previous page: *Brandtfüchse* (fire-foxes/arsonists) and *Hasen* (hares/cowards)</note>, who understand as much about this business as a sow does about spelling—she takes a "D" <note>likely standing for *Dreck* (dirt/dung)</note> for it, as that is healthier for her. Thus you tread the Holy Scripture into the mud; and yet your boasting must count for much here and be spoken as if directly from Heaven. Now look at the testimony of Scripture concerning the destruction of the **Whore** <note>A common Reformation-era reference to the "Whore of Babylon" from the Book of Revelation, interpreted as the corrupt Roman Church</note> and those who also committed adultery with her (for that is also found in **Revelation 2:22**): decide <note>original: *decidiret*</note> upon that, then give your answer, and tell me whether one should enter into a league with the **Papist** and **Roman adulterers**, or rather go out from them (**Revelation 18:4** <note>The text cites verse 5, but the famous command to "Come out of her, my people" is verse 4</note>). Tell me, with what kind of conscience can I side with them, especially when they fight against the Evangelicals? With what kind of conscience can I side with those who resist God in Heaven? How shall I side with the one whom God will finally destroy and root out? Should the **Pope** perish along with those who hold with him—as the Scripture clearly says from God's own mouth—how then must the Devil persuade and ride me, that I should help them against God's command, support them, and on the contrary help to persecute and kill the innocent and the saints of God? Is this now your "beautiful conscience," you fire-foxes, who have actual brands upon your own consciences <note>A pun on *Brandmahl* (a brand or stigma) and *Brandtfüchse*</note>, when I help the Emperor, who is Papist, against the Evangelicals? Could you not, you fire-fox, sit still on your backside and help neither side actively? Yes, but one should "give to Caesar what is Caesar's" <note>A reference to Matthew 22:21</note>. Yes, right! Nothing else should one give him,

...as what is his / and to God also what is God’s <note>A reference to Matthew 22:21: "Render therefore unto Caesar the things which are Caesar's; and unto God the things that are God's."</note>. But is it the right of the **Emperor** that one should help him against the innocent who are devoted to the **Evangelical religion**? Since you think you can maintain a good conscience while siding with the **Papists**, why, you might as well burn all the way down to the Devil along with the **Pope**. Where your conscience remains, God will tell you one day, you desperate **knot-foxes** <note>original: *Knotenfüchse*; a derogatory term for stubborn or deceitful people, likely playing on "knotty" wood or clever foxes</note>; it is desperate, along with the **Pope**, and has become a conscience of straw which will burn on the day of the fierce wrath of God. Oh, how beautifully you take heed of this; how much "good" you have done for them! From the very beginning, people have always been hypocritical, unwilling to do anything that might serve a peaceful settlement, until finally the public, violent proceeding was taken in hand. In the same way, the poor **Bohemians** were not allowed to come to the **Princely College** in **Frankfurt** on the **Election Day** <note>The *Wahltag* of 1619, where Ferdinand II was elected Emperor. The Bohemian delegates were excluded because their revolt against him was ongoing</note>; they were simply cast out and things were made difficult for them. Is that right? Is that fair? Was it done with a good conscience? 

Yes, you **fire-foxes** <note>*Brandfüchse*; a term of abuse for those who incite conflict or are deceitful</note>, I suppose you have done "good" to your fellow believers! You have not only oppressed them together, but also shamefully robbed their belongings and goods, plundered them, bought them up, dragged them away, and carried them into your holes like mice. You bought their cattle from their enemies, shouted a brave "Hurrah!" <note>original: *Heah*</note> over them, and thus made yourselves partakers in everything—sharing the same judgment and sentence with our enemies. How many horses have you stolen and robbed, because of which our land must lie as a wasteland, and yet you must still have something to eat? But hunger will soon snatch you up, and God will not leave you unpunished. 

Is this what it means to have a conscience toward one's fellow believers? If my enemy insults me, says **David** <note>A reference to Psalm 55:12–13: "For it was not an enemy that reproached me; then I could have borne it... But it was thou, a man mine equal, my guide, and mine acquaintance."</note>, then I would endure it; but our own fellow believers are doing this. Where is your conscience now, you **fire-foxes**? You give the **Emperor** what is not his, and you rob God of what is His and give it to the **Emperor**. Is it a good conscience when I offend, overrun, rob, and bleed dry those who have done nothing to me? Is it a conscience that I should be at the service of the godless, acting against my fellow believers who have done nothing to me? If the **Emperor** commanded me to kill his enemy—who was nonetheless my fellow believer—should I do it? Would it be right before God to shed innocent blood, or should I instead become the **Emperor's** hangman and bailiff? Can then such...

...how can such a thing be answered for? If someone has done something to him [the Emperor], he will surely take his own revenge; why must I allow myself to be used in such a matter? Oh no, the saying "Give to the **Emperor** what is the **Emperor’s**" does not apply here at all, for the Scripture speaks about this distinctly. You have become perjurers according to the Scripture against your **fellow believers**, both <term>theologically</term> (in matters of faith) and <term>politically</term> (in matters of state), and yet you defiantly say it is not true; I will explain it to you better. Thus you serve the Devil and his companions, while you bravely help to persecute **God** and His saints—and yet you claim this is done and happens with a good conscience. 

Now, let a sensible person judge what kind of conscience that is, when I outwardly pretend to have a conscience—albeit falsely—while inwardly I violate my conscience? Now the question arises: what is a conscience, and what should one give to the **Emperor**? 

Everyone preserves their conscience when they refrain from doing wrong to anyone against **God’s commandment** and also against the **Law of Nature**, which says: *what you do not want people to do to you, you should also not do to them* <note>The "Golden Rule," derived from Matthew 7:12 and commonly cited in legal philosophy as the *jus naturae*.</note>, and vice-versa, and that one should not make oneself a partaker in the sins of others. Look, that is the good conscience of a **Christian** who fears **God** and loves his fellow believer as himself. Whether these **slanderers** <note>original: *Calumnianten*</note> have done this, the sensible person will recognize. 

Furthermore, one should give to the **Emperor** everything in the outward, political sphere that is right, so far as the inner conscience is not offended against God and the person does not commit a transgression; for the Scripture says, *One should and must obey God more than men*, **Acts 5:29**. <note>original: "Quæ jubet Cæsar, facitis, quæ verò Deus, intermittitis." Translated: "What Caesar commands, you do; but what God [commands], you neglect."</note> 

If the **Emperor** now commanded me to undertake something hostile against another—and indeed, against a fellow **Christian**—it is not at all proper for me to do so, if I wish to avoid staining my conscience with the sins of others and making myself a participant in the deed. Thus far one gives the **Emperor** what is his, and precisely no more; for he is a lord over the outward man, not over the inward conscience. Should I now also make my conscience, which is outwardly obligated, into an inner slave just to please the **Emperor**? 

Oh no, I am dearly bought through the innocent blood of **Jesus Christ**; therefore, I do not serve at all to be pleasing to men, but to **Christ** my own **Lord**. Therefore, *do not become servants of men*—that is, do not serve out of love for men and thereby violate your conscience against **God**, **1 Corinthians 7:23**. The Apostle **Paul** speaks so sharply here, and yet he was only speaking of private matters; how much more should one avoid being drawn into such a general work [of war] in service against one's conscience?

He defines a true Christian conscience as one that refuses to participate in the "foreign sins" of the state.</summary>

[...continued from previous page] how can such a thing be justified? If someone has done something to him <note>Referring to the Emperor</note>, he will surely avenge it; why should I allow myself to be used in it? Oh no, it is not said in vain, "Give to the Emperor what is the Emperor's," for the Scripture speaks *distinctly* <note>original: "distinctè"</note> regarding this. According to the Scripture, you have become perjurers against your fellow believers, both *theologically* <note>original: "Theologicè"</note> and *politically* <note>original: "Politicè"</note>, and you defiantly say it is not true; I will explain it to you better. Thus you serve the Devil and his companions, while you bravely help to persecute God and His saints, and yet you claim this is and should be done with a good conscience. Now, let a sensible person judge what kind of conscience that is, when I outwardly pretend to have a conscience—albeit falsely—and inwardly violate my own conscience?

Now the question is, what then is a conscience, and what should one give to the Emperor? Everyone preserves his conscience when he avoids doing wrong to someone against God's commandment, and also against the Law of Nature <note>A philosophical concept of inherent rights and duties common to all humans.</note>, which says: **What you do not want people to do to you, that you shall also not do to them**, and vice versa <note>original: "& econtra"</note>, and that one should not make oneself a partaker of other men's sins. Behold, that is the good conscience of a Christian who fears God and loves his fellow believer as himself. Whether the slanderers <note>original: "Calumnianten" — those making malicious false accusations.</note> have done this, the sensible person will recognize. 

Furthermore, one should give to the Emperor everything external and political that is right, so long as the inner conscience is not offended against God, and the person does not commit a transgression; for the Scripture says, **"One should and must obey God more than men," Acts 5:29.** <note>original: "Actor. 5. v. 29."</note> "What the Emperor commands, you do; but what God commands, you neglect." <note>original: "Quæ jubet Cæsar, facitis, quæ verò Deus, intermittitis."</note> If the Emperor were to command me to undertake something adverse against another, and specifically against a Christian, it is entirely inappropriate for me, unless I wish to stain my conscience with the sins of others and actually make myself a partaker in the deed. Only to this extent does one give the Emperor what is his, and precisely nothing more; for he is a lord over the external person, not over the inner conscience. Should I now make my conscience, which is outwardly obligated, also an inward slave just to please the Emperor? Oh no, I am dearly bought through the innocent blood of Jesus Christ; therefore, I do not serve at all to be pleasing to men, but to Christ my own Lord. Therefore, "do not become the slaves of men"—that is, do not serve men out of affection and violate your conscience against God, **1 Corinthians 7:23**. Paul the Apostle speaks so sharply, and yet only about private matters; not to mention that one should allow oneself to be used in such a general work for service against the con- <note>The word "conscience" (Gewissen) is split by the page break.</note>

<summary>The author argues that civil obedience to the Emperor is limited by the "Law of Nature" and divine command, particularly when such obedience forces a Christian to harm fellow believers. He defines a true Christian conscience as one that refuses to become a "slave of men" by prioritizing political loyalty over faith.</summary>

how can such a thing be answered for? If someone has done something to him, he will surely avenge it; why should I allow myself to be used for such a purpose? Oh no, it is said here—not at all!—"give to the Emperor what is the Emperor's," for the Scripture speaks distinctly <note>original: "distinctè"</note> about it. You have become perjurers according to the Scripture against your fellow believers, both theologically <note>original: "Theologicè"</note> and politically <note>original: "Politicè"</note>, and yet you defiantly say it is not true; I will explain it to you better. Thus you serve the Devil and his companions, but you bravely help persecute **GOD** and His saints, and yet you claim this should and can be done with a good conscience. Now, let an intelligent person judge what kind of conscience that is, when I outwardly pretend to have a conscience—albeit falsely—while inwardly violating my conscience? 

Now the question is: what then is a conscience, and what should one give to the Emperor? Everyone should guard their conscience whenever they do wrong to someone against God's commandment, and also against the law of nature, which says: **whatever you do not want people to do to you, that you should also not do to them**, and vice versa <note>original: "& econtra"</note>, and that one should not make oneself a partaker in the sins of others. Behold, that is the good conscience of a Christian who fears **GOD** and loves his fellow believer as himself. Whether the slanderers <note>original: "Calumnianten"</note> have done this, the intelligent person will recognize. 

Furthermore, one should give the Emperor all outward political matters that are right, so far and to the extent that the inner conscience is not offended against God, and that man does not overstep. For the Scripture says: "One should and must obey God more than men," *Acts 5, verse 29*. What Caesar commands, you do; what indeed belongs to God, you omit <note>original: "Quæ jubet Cæsar, facitis, quæ verò Deo, intermittitis."</note>. If the Emperor now commanded me to undertake something adverse against another—and indeed against a fellow Christian—it is not at all proper for me to do so, if I do not wish to stain my conscience with the sins of others and effectively make myself a partaker in them. 

Thus far one gives the Emperor what is his, and absolutely nothing more, for he is a lord over the outward man, not over the inward conscience. Should I now take my conscience, which <term>dz</term> <gloss>the scribe uses "dz" as a common abbreviation for "das" (that/which)</gloss> is outwardly bound to duty, and make it also into an inward slave just to please the Emperor? Therefore, "do not become the slaves of men," that is, do not serve out of love for men and thereby violate your conscience against **GOD**, *1 Corinthians 7, verse 23*. ...order a conscience. It is the eternal cry: "Give to the Emperor what is the Emperor's" <note>A reference to Matthew 22:21, often used to justify total obedience to civil authorities</note>. But you bloodhound! Why do you not also demand God's right, and only the Emperor's? These are the worst Pharisees: they demand outward law while abandoning the inward. They enforce the outward with all their might, but do not touch the inward with even a finger. Recognize now what a conscience is, and what the right of the Emperor is.

If we now also decide this question politically <note>original: "Politicè"</note>, there is, alongside the political conscience, a question: can one keep faith and trust (a good conscience) with the Papists <note>A derogatory term for Roman Catholics</note>? To this, the rule of the Papists and Jesuits responds: **Faith need not be kept with heretics** <note>original: "Hæretico non est servanda fides"</note>—one is not obliged to keep loyalty and faith with a heretic. Now, a politician who is sensible would have to be foolish, mad, blind, irrational, and reckless if he wanted to establish a treaty <note>original: "compactat"</note> with the opposing party, since he could not rely on finding an honest man there, but only pure deceit and cunning—as the documented histories and practices of the Papists and Jesuits clearly show. But I have dealt with this question extensively in my previous Decision, to which I refer the sensible reader.

## IX.

### Have the Bohemians then become Calvinist?

There is such a horrific outcry in Meissen <note>A region in Saxony</note> from those shameless slanderers <note>original: "Calumnianten"</note>—it is a shame and a sin—claiming as if all the Bohemians had become Calvinist. But they could not even leave the coinage of the highly praiseworthy King **FREDERICK** <note>Frederick V of the Palatinate, known as the "Winter King" of Bohemia</note> alone; they called it "Calvinist money" and "Calvinist coins," and other such things. Now, I did indeed warn in my previous Decision that such talk should cease, or things would go poorly—I might even have sent you straight to the Devil’s kitchen! But no, it did no good; therefore, I will properly shine a light on you, you firebrands <note>original: "brandFüchse," a term for inflammatory deceivers or young scoundrels</note>, so that it shall sting you to the heart.

First of all, since I indeed represent <unclear>jestagire</unclear> <note>likely "act as" or "play the part of"</note> a Palatine steward <note>original: "Truckseß," a high court official responsible for the table</note> and also a Bohemian cupbearer <note>original: "Mundschencken"</note>, I must first taste the wine <note>original: "credenzen," the act of testing food/drink for poison before serving</note> from the overflowing cup; afterwards, I will give it to **Doctor Hoë** <note>Matthias Hoë von Hoënegg, a staunch Lutheran advisor to the Elector of Saxony and a fierce critic of Calvinism</note> and let him carouse along with his slanderers.

I therefore ask the Doctor: does he even know what "Calvinist" means? "Oh yes," he says, "should I not know it? I, who have written so much against the Calvinists and especially recently collected ninety-nine articles against them." Well, that is good; for this time, we want to take just a single article out of those same ninety-nine "High Articles" and present it to your Grace: namely, whether good works are necessary for salvation.

Doctor Mas <note>Possibly a reference to a specific Lutheran polemicist, or a generic name for a dogmatic opponent</note> says that this idea is Turkish, Arian, Calvinist, and Muhammadan <note>These were common 17th-century insults used to label an opponent's views as "infidel" or "heretical"</note>; for Paul says that faith alone justifies. Paul speaks rightly! But the question is: what kind of faith is it? Thus the Apostle himself decides and says in Galatians 5:6: **In Christ Jesus, neither circumcision nor uncircumcision counts for anything, but FAITH which is ACTIVE through love.** Furthermore, faith must take root and be established through love. How is it then? Why does he chatter so much and say that faith alone justifies without the works of the Law (Romans 3)? 

Yes, you poor devil! It is correct; the phrase is "without the works of the Law"—namely, that whoever is under faith is not under the Law, as he says elsewhere. But is the Law or the works therefore abolished through faith? Oh no, Mr. Doctor, far from it! Rather, it is upheld all the more through a true, powerful faith (Romans 3:31). 

For whoever says they believe in Christ and does not love Him, let them be accursed <note>original: "anathema"</note>, banned <note>original Hebrew: "Maharam," referring to the ban or *herem*</note>, and dead <note>original: "mota"</note> (1 Corinthians 16:22). Thus, works never come from the Law, nor from the Law's or human ability, but from faith, out of faith, and occurring through Christ in us (Philippians 1:11), as the Scripture clearly testifies. 

Now we ask: are good works effectively <note>original: "effectivè"</note> necessary for salvation? Yes, in every way! For **repentance, loving, praying, petitioning, and obeying** are indeed good works. Now, it is impossible for one to be saved without repentance and without obedience—which obedience is nothing other than the working and active faith. Concerning this, the Son of God himself speaks of faith to the Jews in John 6: "This is doing the work of God: that you believe in the one he has sent." But all these works happen powerfully in us through Christ himself, as the Scripture says, and not out of our own ability; for we contribute nothing to it except to obey, according to our free will, when our conscience preaches repentance to us; and that is called our own faith, which obedience and faith become active...

Oh, God forbid! You will say that is an enormous claim, that I must be mistaken or have dreamed it. You surely stand as a **theologian**, namely, just as if you were to graduate at **Wittenberg**; there, one had to let the poor **weeping candidate** <note>original: *Doctorantem lachrymantem* — a reference to a student who perhaps struggled through his exams or begged for mercy from the faculty</note> enter and pass through special intercession, because he made a fool of **Dr. Polycarp** <note>Likely Polykarp Leyser the Elder (1552–1610), a prominent Lutheran theologian in Saxony and Hoe’s predecessor</note> with his nonsense until he had his **doctorate** in hand; then it all became worthless rubbish.

But now the **Doctor** starts all over again; he issues **NINETY-NINE** articles of lies and breeches <note>The author is punning on Matthias Hoe’s famous "99 Articles" against the Calvinists. By calling them *Hosenartickel* (breeches articles), he compares Hoe’s theological points to common pieces of clothing</note>; he has certainly not learned enough that he could have patched even one more onto them. But who will not notice the lofty spirit of the puffed-up **Doctor** in those **Ninety-nine**? It is a brave and impressive word, more so than "a hundred"; just like someone who buys himself ninety-nine yards of fabric for a pair of breeches, so it is with **Doctor Hoe** and his 99 breeches articles. So that all the articles might be made full, I will grant this question to the **Doctor**, so that other articles may become full; he may help himself with that until I also provide him with the remaining rest.

Someone might say, however, that I am also **Calvinist** because I speak this way: No! I am what I am through the grace of God. I name and profess myself after no man or human name, for that is against the **Scripture**. But what is right, I search out through the Spirit of God, bring it forth, and testify to the truth; for I have been sent and called to this by God, no matter whom it may vex, and regardless of what anyone may think of me. For I know well that I am not worthy of the least honor, nor do I desire any status; but in the zeal of the Spirit, I demand the right of Truth and the honor of Christ. But if anyone else has a desire to **examine** me, let him just do it; I will be fine and am already prepared through the grace of Christ to give an answer and an account of my faith.

Finally, regarding this question, let **Doctor Hoe** take note of what I mean: Namely, that every single one of his Articles of Faith—none excepted—will in the future be so sharply **examined** that the like has never happened at any time. If he is such a brave knight, let him get ready, along with all his **doctors, professors,** and **superintendents**; let him be their **General**. But the grace of Christ will now be powerful in me, and then we shall deliver a grand battle to one another, like **Samson with the thousand Philistines**. What is it worth? God will also live and remain in **Bohemia**, and the **Meisseners** <note>The people of Meissen, representing the Saxon Lutheran establishment</note> will not have swallowed all the arts and wisdom. Bless you, Father! Go on then; we **Bohemians** received the **Gospel** from you.

## X.
### Whether the Bohemians alone have sinned, and what kind of thanks those will receive who side with the Papists? <note>original: *Papisten* — a derogatory term for Roman Catholics</note>

THESE miserable **slanderers** and the world at large are so blind that they all together cry out only against the **Bohemians**, only against the **Bohemians**, saying how horribly they have trespassed against the **Emperor**, and consequently <note>original: *Consequenter*</note> also against **God**; and that for this reason, God has punished them so that they are now entirely conquered and have become a mockery and a scorn to the whole world. They claim no sin could be as great as that of the **Bohemians**, simply because they, along with their incorporated lands, have been so terribly punished by God and the high authorities. Indeed, many a man might think it were so, but what will the **Prophetic decision** <note>The author refers here to a definitive ruling from Scripture</note> tell us? In **Luke 13**, the **LORD** speaks thus:

> Do you think that these **Galileans** were worse sinners than all the other **Galileans** because they suffered such things? Or do you think those **eighteen** who died when the **tower in Siloam** fell on them and killed them were more guilty than all the others living in **Jerusalem**? I tell you, no! But unless you repent, you too will all perish.

Hear now, you mockers, what the **LORD** says there: namely, how one should not rejoice in the misfortune of his neighbor, nor revile, mock, and utterly condemn him, as the **Meißner** <note>People from the Margraviate of Meissen in Saxony, here referring to the Saxon Lutherans who supported the Emperor against the Bohemian Protestants</note> do to us **Bohemians**. For we are all sinners and fail in many ways; but our neighbors justify themselves over us, scolding us as willful enemies, wishing for us to be struck on the cheeks, to have our teeth pulled, sneering at us and the like. Where they, as **theologians**, should wish us a repentant heart—if indeed we have sinned—and what is more, we have never been enemies of the **Meißner**. But now **Christ** desires that one should do good even to enemies, and especially to fellow believers. Behold your understanding, modeled after the "understanding" of the **great theologian in Dresden** <note>Matthias Hoe von Hoenegg</note>, and his love toward his fellow believers; he is supposed to teach others, and yet he himself does the very thing...

## IX.

### Whether and how the Bohemians and the Kingdom can be compared to Jerusalem, and how it is likewise compared through the Wisdom of God?

When the **slanderers** <term>slanderers: original *Calumnianten*, referring to those spreading false or malicious reports against the Bohemian rebels</term> write something, they do it according to "fools' law"—namely, they take nothing but pure outward fantasies and monkey-business, making a spectacle of it, no different than the **Jesuits** with their comedies; it is nothing but monkey-nonsense without foundation or wisdom. But it behooves an intelligent **actor** <note>original: *Actori* (Latin); here meaning a pleader or one who performs a public role</note> to arrange everything wisely so that he may stand firm and not become a laughingstock. Thus should everyone do who wishes to act rightly and drive out his enemies, so that he is not considered a braying fool by the wise and laughed at.

It is, however, for the wise to know how the state of **Jerusalem** in the Old Testament is a true **type** <term>type: a person, place, or event in the Old Testament seen as a prophetic symbol or "shadow" of something in the New Testament or the present</term> and model of the Jerusalem in the New Testament. The prophetic predictions extend in like manner even to our own time, according to the secret wisdom of God, as experience will demonstrate.

### XII.

->How it then fared and will fare with Jerusalem,<-
->and how it will later go for other nations:<-

When we look at Jerusalem because of her great sin and misdeed, she is compared in Holy Scripture to Sodom, Ezekiel 8. I will distress Ariel, so that there shall be heaviness and sorrow, and it shall be unto me a true Ariel," (one who is white and swims in red blood). <note>The author provides a grizzly interpretation of the heraldic "White Lion" of Bohemia being stained red by the blood of the Thirty Years' War.</note> "For I will camp against thee round about, and will lay siege against thee with a mount, and I will raise forts against thee. And thou shalt be brought down, and shalt speak out of the ground, and thy speech shall be low out of the dust, and thy voice shall be, as of one that hath a familiar spirit <note>original: *Zauberers*, meaning a sorcerer or necromancer</note> out of the ground, and thy speech shall whisper out of the dust. And the multitude of those who scatter thee shall be like small dust, and the multitude of the tyrants like chaff that passeth away; and it shall happen suddenly, in an instant"—Note Well!—(that is, it shall happen very hastily and unexpectedly, and be quickly accomplished). "For thou shalt be visited by the LORD of Hosts <note>original: *HERRN Zebaoth*</note> with thunder, and with earthquake, and great noise, with storm and tempest, and the flame of devouring fire." But those who distress Ariel shall become **FOOLS** <note>original: **NARREN**</note> over it, for it will also apply to them, etc. Let every sensible person mark this well, for things will suddenly and soon become different; therefore, consider these prophetic words—not one shall fail to be fulfilled <term>adæquirte<note>from the Latin *adaequare*: to be made equal to, or to match perfectly. ### XIII.

# How religion will be constituted in the 
# future?

Since it is revealed through the secret and prophetic wisdom of **GOD** that all nations shall be severely punished by **GOD**, the person of understanding might well wish to know how it will then go and stand with religion and the Gospel? If we wish to make a completely precise and correct <term>decision <note>original: "decisionem"</note></term> on this matter, we must once again ask the Prophets and no one else. They will clearly specify for us where and in which land first of all the true Gospel shall be, and where it shall be preached and spread from there into all lands before the final day of this world.

Thus the Prophets, namely Isaiah in chapter 2 and Micah, clearly say and prophesy to us concerning the end times: It shall come to pass in the last days that the mountain where the **LORD’S** house is shall be established, higher than all mountains, and exalted above all hills; and all heathens shall flow unto it, and many people shall go and say, "Come, let us go up to the mountain of the **LORD**, to the house of the **GOD** of Jacob, that He may teach us His ways, and we may walk in His paths." For out of Zion shall go forth the law, and the **LORD’S** word from Jerusalem, etc. This text and prophecy speaks properly and clearly, in express words, of the end times. ## XIV.

### How it stands with the Roman Eagle, which is now with the House of Austria, and otherwise with the House of Austria, according to the secret wisdom of GOD?

I am exceedingly amazed how it must always be with <term>Doctor Hoë <note>Matthias Hoë von Hoënegg (1580–1645), a high-ranking Lutheran court preacher in Saxony known for his fierce polemics against both Calvinists and mystics</note></term> and other slanderers, that they are so completely stone-blind and can understand neither themselves nor the Prophet <term>Daniel</term> and the <term>Apocalypse <note>The Book of Revelation</note></term> regarding the state of the Roman Empire and the Eagle. ...and afterward the interpretation is explained again. In verse 23, the angel speaks: The Fourth Beast will be the Fourth Kingdom on earth, which will be mightier than all kingdoms; it will devour all lands, tread them down, and crush them. The Ten Horns signify ten kings who will arise from that same kingdom. But after them, another shall arise who will be more powerful than any of the previous ones, and he will humble three kings. He will blaspheme the Most High, and destroy the saints of the Most High, and will attempt to change times and laws. They (namely the saints) will be given into his hand for a time, times, and half a time <note>A prophetic period often interpreted as three and a half years, or here, centuries</note>. After that, judgment shall be held, and then his power will be taken away so that he is utterly destroyed and killed. But the kingdom, power, and authority under the whole heaven will be given to the holy people of the Most High, whose kingdom is eternal, and all power shall serve him. That was the end of the speech. We want to examine these words.

First, the angel speaks of the beast, and then he comes to the **little horn**, which carries the power of the beast and becomes as great as all the others next to it, so that it even humbles three kings. Now, this **little horn** is none other than the **House of Austria**, which had its beginning from **RUDOLPH I** <note>original: "RUDOLPHO primo"</note>, a Count of Habsburg, who in the **YEAR** of Christ 1273, when there was such a vacancy <note>The Great Interregnum, a period with no recognized Emperor</note> in the Empire, was elected as the Roman Emperor. When his emperorship was confirmed in the **YEAR** 1275, he made his son **ALBERT** <note>original: "ALBERTUM"</note> an Archduke in Austria, and thus elevated him above other princes and dukes. 

In the following time, the power of this House grew so high and became so strong that for three hundred and fifty years <note>original: "Vierdhalb Hundert Jahren," literally four-less-a-half hundred</note> they have been the most prominent princes; and also during this named time, most of the Emperors have been from the House of Austria, and thus the Beast of the **Fourth Monarchy** <note>The Fourth Monarchy refers to the Roman Empire, which many contemporary scholars believed survived through the Holy Roman Empire</note>. Thus the prophecy of the angel has been rightly fulfilled. For at first, this House was a **Little Horn**—that is, a Count—but afterward it became an Emperor, Archduke, Emperor and King, and finally was elevated so high that no prince in the Empire could ever reach so high. This **little horn** has thus humbled the **three kings**; namely, because a single person from the House of Austria has always been three kinds of king, or a triple king: namely, **Roman**, **Hungarian**, and **Bohemian King**. Otherwise, in ancient times, there was indeed a Roman King alongside the Roman Emperor.

...existed. But finally, it has all come down to a single person—that is, to become so great as no one ever was, and greater than the others who are alongside him. And what is even more, the Kings of Spain <note>original: "Hispanien"</note> are also from the **House of Austria**. Who would not say now that this **little horn** has grown? Indeed, it has been mightier than any of those who came before.

Now the angel also describes the "beautiful" virtues and deeds <note>The author uses "beautiful" sarcastically to describe what he considers villainous acts</note> that this horn has performed and done: namely, blaspheming the Most High, distracting and destroying his saints, even overcoming them, and undertaking to change times and laws. All of this has indeed been fulfilled, for there has not been a single day when an Emperor from the **House of Austria** did not adhere to the **Pope** <note>original: "Pabst"</note> and thus blaspheme God. Likewise, no Emperor has ever so destroyed the saints of the Most High (by "the saints" <term>Heiligen</term> we mean Bohemia, the Incorporated Lands, as well as Styria and Austria, etc., and in general all persecuted **Evangelicals** <note>The term "Evangelisch" refers to Protestants, particularly Lutherans and Calvinists</note> in the Netherlands and elsewhere) as this very House and horn has; and finally, according to the prophecy, it has even maintained the victory over them. What unheard-of destruction has the Spaniard driven against the saints of God? In all eternity, the Turk <note>A reference to the Ottoman Empire, often used as a benchmark for cruelty in this era</note> has not treated Christians as the cursed Spaniard has, who is certainly also of this little blaspheming horn in his lineage. This House has also undertaken, alongside the Pope, to **change the time** when it commanded the **new calendar** <note>The Gregorian Calendar, introduced by Pope Gregory XIII in 1582, was rejected by many Protestants for decades as a papist invention</note> and ordered it within the Empire, and has otherwise given all sorts of statutes and laws. Now we know clearly enough how it stands with this **little horn**, who it actually is, and what it carries on its shield. Now we want to perform the calculation and see how long it will remain in its *existence* <note>original: "esse"</note>, and when the saints of the Most High shall rule.

The angel speaks of a **time, times, and half a time**. Now, in **Prophetic Arithmetic**, there are three kinds of "times": a great one, which is 1,000 years; a middle one, which is 100 years; and a small one, which is 10 years. <note>original Latin: "Adde tempus quartum quod constat uno tantum anno, uti exempl. Apoc. 12. v. 14." — Add a fourth time which consists of only one year, as in the example of Revelation 12, verse 14.</note> Here, however, if I take the "great time," it would be too much, and the "small time" too little; therefore, I must take the "middle time" and count: one "time" is 100; "times" (or two times) is 200; "half a time" is 50. That makes 350 years. Now I take these 350 years, as they are indicated here by the angel through the "times," and add them to 1273, which is the time and **Year of Christ** <note>original: "Annus Christi"</note>...

->XV.<-

### Who then has overcome in the Bohemian affair: the Emperor, or the Bohemians with their King, according to secret understanding?

When we look upon the matter with secret wisdom <note>A reference to mystical or "theosophical" insight into historical events.</note>, we find precisely what the Scripture says, namely: how all things must work for the best for those who love God.
<margin>Romans 8.</margin>
That the Bohemians and their King <note>Frederick V, the "Winter King," who was defeated by the Imperial forces at the Battle of White Mountain in 1620.</note> have now been overcome—the mocker knows how to make use of that fact as much as anything. But the wise know something far different, for it is indeed true and right to say: *Patientia vincit* <note>original: "Patientia vincit," Latin for "Patience overcomes."</note>. Patience overcomes, even if the mockers do not wish to understand it. We have an excellently beautiful example of this and similar overcoming in our Savior and Lord Christ Jesus, who, precisely through his death—as the truly heavenly, crafty serpent—overcame the old serpent, the Dragon. If the Devil was a crafty serpent, then Christ was even craftier, so much so that the Devil became stone-blind and a fool because of it. 

He alone may be a true hero, 
Who fells his enemy with cunning, 
When he goes willingly into death, 
And brings his enemy into highest need. 

As it went for Christ, so it goes for his saints; for in being killed, their death is nothing other than the death, poison, healing, and pestilence of their enemies. For the saints are not outwardly, but inwardly crafty, which the poor Devil cannot see. Indeed, the Papists themselves must confess that the more Evangelical blood is shed

<meta>catchword: the more</meta>

Furthermore, now that the Bohemians have been overcome, everyone cried out: "Where is their God now? Surely the Papist religion must be right because the Emperor won; for if the Bohemians had the true faith, God would have rescued them." But no, my lords! One must not speak so foolishly of God, for He is a wondrous and hidden God. When He intends something great, He makes it so strange in our eyes that the world becomes mad and foolish—indeed, quite idiotic—over it. God does this so that He might bring to nothing and utterly shame the understanding of the world-wise and the clever, so that the worldly scholars finally become a mockery and a scorn. For thus says the Scripture: 

<margin>1 Corinthians 1</margin>
"I will destroy the wisdom of the wise, and the understanding of the prudent I will cast away. Where are the clever? Where are the scholars? Where are the philosophers of this world? Has not God made the wisdom of this world into foolishness?"

Therefore, God does not call for this business anyone wise according to the flesh—not the mighty or the noble, such as the high doctors are—but rather one who is wretched, poor, simple, and foolish before the world. This is whom God has chosen, so that God may put the wise to shame and catch them in their own great cleverness. Then, when the Word of the Lord is fulfilled, you mockers might as well go along with Doctor Max in Dresden <note>Likely referring to Matthias Hoë von Hoënegg, the influential court preacher in Dresden who was famously anti-Calvinist and supported the Catholic Emperor against the Protestant Bohemians.</note> and cry out, "O, how right it serves the Bohemians, for they have committed treason!" <note>original: "Crimen læsæ Majestatis," the legal term for high treason or violating the majesty of the sovereign.</note> O, you blind ones! Why will you not let your eyes be anointed by Christ with eye-salve? Do you not know what He says to you Laodiceans? <note>A reference to the "lukewarm" church in the Book of Revelation, warned for its spiritual blindness.</note> Have I not also faithfully warned you on God’s behalf in my *Mirror of the Times* <note>original: "ZeitSpiegell"</note> and previous *Decision*? How is it that you still refuse to be advised? Well then, now expect something else, and go on treating Christ and His servants as fools once more.

This, then, is the state of the Bohemian business—not as the world-clever suppose, but as the counsel of the Lord is; even so...

And let this be said to you by me, who am a fool before the world, as if spoken by a fool; for as the common proverb gladly says: **Children and fools speak the truth.** It will not be long now, and things will soon change; then one will certainly and in fact experience who was right or wrong, who won or lost, who was overcome, or who has overcome.

## XVI.

### Whether—to speak theologically and simply—the
### Bohemians are in the wrong because they defended
### themselves against the authorities, and whether a
### Christian should wage war?

As to whether the Bohemians were so completely right in their resistance, that is not the case [simply], namely if I consider them as private persons. For a Christian, for himself alone, and making a distinction...

<summary>Felgenhauer defends his partisan support of the Bohemians as a spiritual necessity, rebuking neutral "hypocrites" and the Emperor's advisors, before opening a theological debate on the right of resistance and Christian participation in war.</summary>
<keywords>Bohemian Revolt, partisanship, Revelation 3, Matthias Hoe von Hoenegg, resistance theory, Christian warfare, religious polemic</keywords>

--- Page 102 ---
<meta>This page continues Felgenhauer’s theological defense of the Bohemian revolt and the concept of "Just War" for Christians. It specifically addresses the tension between the Christian command for non-violence and the duty of the state to protect its citizens. The catchword "dem" from the previous page suggests the sentence structure: "...and [to him who] fears..."</meta>

[whoever] fears / should not wage war / indeed he cannot / but should suffer gladly / as one who is patient / for otherwise there would be no martyrs / if someone wanted to defend himself against the enemy of his faith. But if I speak of this *collectively* <note>original: "collectivè"; referring to the church or community as a whole rather than an individual.</note> / then a Christian certainly has the power / to wage war / especially when he is appointed as an **authority** <term>Obrigkeit: the divinely ordained secular government or magistracy.</term> / who is meant to protect the oppressed / for she does not carry the sword in vain / but for vengeance upon the wicked / so that they do not utterly suppress the pious. In this way, one authority fights against another / when the ungodly person intends to suppress the miserable with violence. Thus also the praiseworthy Estates in **Bohemia** were likewise *Defenders of the Fatherland* <note>original: "Defensores patriæ"</note> / an authority / who also carries the sword. For what a foolish thing it would be / if the **Beast** <note>A common Reformation-era reference to the Papacy or the Antichrist, as described in the Book of Revelation.</note> should hold a conflict with the Saints / if they / the Saints / did not also fight; what need would there be for much fighting and overcoming / if one does not defend oneself? One takes a person by the head / and deals with him as one pleases / if one does not defend oneself. But where there is fighting / there the victory lingers through the struggle for so long / until finally one part is overcome by the other. Thus it was certainly granted to the Saints by GOD / to wage war with the Beast / and why should they be in the wrong for this? For when Christ says one should not defend oneself / He speaks of **private persons** / and not of the authority / to whom He ordained the sword / and placed it in their hand. A private person may well answer for themselves with Christ / against the one who strikes them / and does them wrong / but they should not strike back / but rather leave the vengeance primarily to the authority or to God / who will surely avenge it in His time / but you, meanwhile, do all good things for your enemy / For precisely by your doing good / you do him the greatest and most harmful evil / namely / when you heap **fiery coals** upon his head / which will burn in his soul.

There is a *dispute* <note>original: "disputat"</note> among the learned as to whether a Christian should wage war? Look, there you have the *decision* <note>original: "Decision"</note> / and do not think / it is wrong / to wage war / for you must distinguish correctly / then you will also teach correctly. If Christians did not defend themselves against the **Pope** and the **Turks** / they would have devoured them all long ago. This is also what the **Papists** <note>A polemical term for Roman Catholics.</note> have done / pushing our **Evangelical** <note>Protestant</note> Estates out of their offices / issuing unmerited orders against them / holding them up to them / and in the process they were caught in their injustice / and were **thrown out of the window** <note>A direct reference to the Defenestration of Prague (1618), the event that sparked the Thirty Years' War, which Felgenhauer here defends as a legitimate act of the Bohemian Estates.</note>. Yes, say the *slanderers* <note>original: "Calumnianten"</note>, if something had been too difficult / then one should have

### XVII.

### Whether Doctor Hoë also did wrong by becoming a Count Palatine?

He, **Doctor Hoë** <note>Matthias Hoë von Hoënegg (1580–1645), a powerful and controversial Lutheran court preacher in Saxony who, despite being Protestant, often supported the Catholic Holy Roman Emperor against other Protestant factions.</note>, writes in one place that he longs to hear what sin he has committed by accepting and receiving the **title of Count Palatine** <note>original: "Comitatum Palatinatus". This refers to the title of Imperial Count Palatine (Comes Palatinus Caesareus), an honorary rank bestowed by the Emperor that granted the recipient certain legal powers.</note>. Now, my dear neighbor, wait just a moment; I will tell it to you quite clearly, and in good plain German, how the matter stands—and not with foolish jests out of my own head, but based firmly on the foundation of the Scriptures, from the **Book of Revelation** <note>original: "Apocalypsi"</note>.

In the **Book of Revelation**, the **Beast**—which is the **Roman Empire** <note>original: "Römische Keyserthumb". Early modern Protestant reformers often identified the "Beast" of Revelation with the Papacy or the Holy Roman Empire.</note>, as Doctor Hoë himself interprets it—is described in all its circumstances. Those who side with it are also described; but on the other hand, all people are most solemnly warned to have nothing to do with its **marks** <note>The "Mark of the Beast."</note>. For all those who worship, honor, and praise it, and submit themselves to it, taking its mark, are not written in the **Book of Life**, etc. The Doctor may well have read such a warning, but how he understood it and allowed himself to be warned is shown by his own actions. For he signs himself as the Emperor's "most humble," indeed "most submissive and dutiful" servant. Here he praises the Emperor so excessively that it is nearly impossible to believe, and he lets it be known to everyone how one ought to honor the Emperor, saying: *Who is like unto him, and who can make war with him?* <note>A direct quote from Revelation 13:4, where the people of the earth worship the Beast, saying "Who is like unto the beast? who is able to make war with him?" The author is accusing Hoë of using the language of idolatry for the Emperor.</note>

If he wanted to be a sensible **theologian**, the Devil would not ride him so. For it is well known how that little "Beast" has behaved toward God and His saints. Or shall it not be true, what I have testified until now from the witness of **Jesus Christ**? If so, then God must judge you, you who give the lie to His mouth and help to protect the unjust. Yes, but Doctor Hoë says, or might say, that he honored the Emperor **politically** <note>original: "Politicè"</note>—outwardly only—and not from the heart as a true Christian. Quite right; for such a **government** <note>original: "Politiam"</note> as the Emperor leads, so shall he also be honored. Yet Doctor Hoë did it from the bottom of his heart, and by no means falsely, with the...

It means nothing else than that one calls himself after the beast and its name, is exempted and privileged by him, allows himself to be appointed against God and against the saints, and does nothing but shout and bray, "Emperor, Emperor, Emperor!" Meanwhile, God may be wherever He wishes with His saints, so long as one gives the Emperor what is not his, and in return takes from God what is His. It means to boast greatly of the Emperor—how one is an **Imperial Commissioner** <note>original: "Keyserlicher Commissarius"</note> over the Bohemians, how one was made a **Count Palatine** <note>original: "Comes Palatinatus"</note> by the Emperor because of the "faithful services" performed for him, and more of such filth.

Does this not mean worshipping the beast and its image, and acknowledging oneself bound to serve the beast, whereby God and His saints are blasphemed and shamed? See, you dragon-servants and beast-worshippers, how you serve the devil and blaspheme God in heaven along with the beast. Now read what your wage is, and enjoy it with the beast and the dragon, since you would not allow yourselves to be warned.

In the **Apocalypse**, the beast—which is the Roman Empire <note>original: "Römische Keyserthumb"</note> / as Doctor Hoë himself interprets it—is clearly described in all its circumstances / and those who side with it are also described / But on the other hand, it most dearly warns all people / that they should have nothing to do with its mark / for all those who worship it / honor / praise / and surrender themselves to it / taking its mark / they are not written in the Book of Life / etc. Such a warning the Doctor may well have read / but how he understood it / and whether he let himself be warned / he testifies himself / For he signs himself as the Emperor's most humble / yes, most submissive / and most duty-bound <unclear>servant</unclear> <note>OCR reads "Kiehs," likely a corruption of "Knecht" (servant).</note> / he praises the Emperor so excessively / as much as is possible / and also lets it be known to everyone / how one should honor that Emperor / and says: Who is like unto him / and who can wage war with him? <note>A direct echo of Revelation 13:4, where the people wonder at the power of the Beast.</note> If he wanted to be a sensible theologian / then the Devil should not ride him so / For it is indeed known / how that little beast has behaved / toward God and His saints / Or shall it not be true / what I have testified from the witness of Jesus Christ until now?
